# Build Log

_Pre-write contract. Every Write/Edit to `dashboard/` is preceded by one line here. Empty contract → no write allowed. This file is the audit trail Claude commits to and Luneth verifies._

Format: `[YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM <TZ>] surface · concern · file(s) touched · rationale` — `<TZ>` = the machine's current local zone; read it from the clock, never hardcode (it drifted back to a stale "EDT" after the move to Central).

Reverse-chronological is fine (newest at top after the header) — read top-down to see what changed most recently.
